| D-261 | **FBV2-P2-002O: D-260'S RIGID-CLUSTER HYPOTHESIS IS CONFIRMED, AND THE KELVIN BLOCKER IS NOW CLOSED ARITHMETIC THAT MISSES BY 0.132 mm. DECISION STOP; the authoritative stackup was NOT changed**, because section 17 conditions the lock on section 14's combined gate. The PCB is byte-identical to `fcacf0e` (md5 `a908cedfa9f9410aab327d8bd55b9f45`) - **four copper layers, zero signal tracks, zero signal vias** - and all five suites PASS. **THE HYPOTHESIS IS RIGHT ABOUT THE CAUSE:** 002N moved R80/R81/R82 WITHOUT U18 and three control connections failed against `BAT_MAIN routed clearance 0.3000 mm` at 0.2750, 0.2778 and 0.2371 mm, taking U18 from 8 of 8 to 6 of 8. With U18 and R76..R83 moved TOGETHER as one rigid body **those three failures do not recur** - the relative geometry between U18's pins and its own divider ring is what every 8-of-8 result since 002M depended on, exactly as D-260 read it. **IT SIMPLY CANNOT MOVE FAR ENOUGH, AND THAT IS NOW ARITHMETIC RATHER THAN A SEARCH RESULT.** R75 at rot 0/180 - the only orientation that gives Kelvin symmetry - has a 3.875 mm courtyard half-width and therefore needs a **7.75 mm corridor**; the space between the board-edge clearance and the divider column is **6.80 mm**. West limit: the pad west edge must clear 0.500 mm, so **R75.x >= 4.075**. East limit by cluster shift: **+0.00 -> x < 3.425; +0.50 -> x < 3.925 (AN EMPTY WINDOW); +0.75 -> x < 4.175, a 0.100 mm window that finally opens; +1.00 -> x < 4.425.** **And only cluster +0.50 mm is legal**: at +0.75 and +1.00 **D9 overlaps R77 by 0.170 mm and 0.420 mm** respectively, while TP17 (R76) and C58 (R78/R79) appear only from +1.25. **SECTION 13'S TEST-POINT SCAN THEREFORE ANSWERS CLEARLY: the obstruction is D9 - a FUNCTIONAL diode in the protected high-current path - and NOT a test point.** No test point was moved and none would help. **THE ALTERNATIVE ORIENTATION WAS RE-MEASURED WITH THE CLUSTER MOVED: rot 90/270 bottoms out at 5.132 mm mismatch against the 5.000 mm limit** (002N measured 5.177 mm unmoved; the +0.50 mm shift bought 0.045 mm of it), and that pose was ROUTED anyway to see what the rest of the gate does with it: **U18 4 of 8**, with `LTC_SHDN` NO_LEGAL_ESCAPE, `LTC4368_FAULT_N` NO_VIA_SITE and `LTC_UV`/`LTC_OV` NO_PATH - moving R75 east far enough to improve the mismatch puts the shunt under U18's own escape corridor. **So both routes to the Kelvin specification miss by less than two tenths of a millimetre, and every lever this task holds has been pulled.** **WHAT DID CLOSE: `LTC_OV R77.2 -> R78.1` routed 2.901 mm on B.Cu with ZERO vias** - the section 11 requirement, met, on the first candidate that reached it and **without moving R77 at all** - and **section 11's fallback lock is now enforced in the harness**: `AQROOT_LTCOV_BCU` denies LTC_OV and LTC_UV the generic layer fallback during qualification, so 002N's outcome of 'one connected component, via 13.087 mm across F.Cu with two vias' **can no longer be reported as a pass**. **TWO TOOLING DEFECTS FOUND AND FIXED.** First, the pose filter tested courtyards against the board OUTLINE and nothing else, and proposed R75 at x = 3.900 rot 180 whose west pad sits **0.325 mm** from the edge against a **0.500 mm** board-setup clearance; the screen rejected EVERY connection from the first one and routed nothing at all, so the edge rule now applies to the PADS - a filter that admits a pose the board rejects outright costs a whole screen. Second, the placement-identity guard refused a candidate because the file said `270.0` and KiCad reports `-90.0` - **the same pose**; angles are now compared modulo 360, because **a guard that fires on a difference that is not a difference trains people to ignore it**, and this guard exists precisely because FBV2-P2-002K ran nine screens on the wrong placement. **DECISION REQUIRED, and all three options are outside this task's authority: (a) AUTHORISE A 0.170 mm EASTWARD MOVE OF D9**, which admits the +0.75 mm cluster shift, opens the 0.100 mm window for R75 rot 180 and yields a Kelvin mismatch of about 0.10 mm - a protection-architecture call, since D9 sits in the high-current path; **(b) OR RELAX THE KELVIN MISMATCH from 5.000 to 5.132 mm**, noting that the rot 90/270 pose which achieves it also costs four U18 control pins, so this alone does NOT close the gate; **(c) OR CHANGE THE SHUNT** - R75's **5.925 mm pad pitch** is simultaneously what pins the rot 90/270 mismatch near the shunt's own length and what makes the rot 0/180 corridor 7.75 mm wide, so a physically shorter 15 mOhm part of the same rating relieves BOTH at once; that is a component decision rather than a placement one, and it is raised, not taken. **U19 NOT SEARCHED** (section 20); Phase A NOT run; no battery signal copper written; converter routing NOT started. B-34 REMAINS OPEN. Full analysis: [`audits/2026-08-26-p2-002o-d260-cluster.md`](audits/2026-08-26-p2-002o-d260-cluster.md). **NO PROGRESS EARNED: PCB routing stays 0 %, overall stays 74 %.** | 2026-08-26 |
| D-262 | **FBV2-P2-002P: D-261'S D9 LEVER WORKS, `BAT_SENSE` CLOSES, AND THE GATE FAILS FOR A DIFFERENT REASON. DECISION STOP under section 17; the authoritative stackup was NOT changed**, because section 19 conditions the lock on section 16. The PCB is byte-identical to `b803f93` (md5 `a908cedfa9f9410aab327d8bd55b9f45`) - **four copper layers, zero signal tracks, zero signal vias** - and all five suites PASS. **THE D9 RELOCATION IS AUTHORISED, BOUNDED AND MEASURED: 0.600 mm EAST, PLACEMENT ONLY** - same part number, footprint, orientation, net, polarity and topology, exactly as D-261 ruled. What each displacement buys was measured rather than assumed: **+0.20 and +0.40 mm free only cluster +0.75; +0.60 mm is the MINIMUM that frees cluster +1.00**, and at +1.25 mm the cluster is blocked by TP17 and C58 regardless of D9. **`D9.1 -> U11.2` SHORTENS from 55.344 to 54.748 mm**, so the protected path does not grow. **THE CORRIDOR OPENS AND THE KELVIN SPECIFICATION BECOMES SATISFIABLE FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE 002I:** with D9 at +0.600 and the rigid cluster at +1.00, R75 rot 180 has a legal window of **x 4.225 .. 4.400** in which the **Kelvin mismatch reaches 0.000 mm** with both branches at **7.321 mm** against a 10 mm cap. **AND `BAT_SENSE Q3.6 -> R75.1` - THE BLOCKER STANDING SINCE FBV2-P2-002M - IS CLOSED**: 1.00 mm on B.Cu, **zero vias**, one functional island. On the best candidate - **D9 (13.600, 72.500), cluster +0.75 mm, R75 (4.150, 63.500) rot 180** - the probe returns **all nine targets true with U18 8 of 8**, `LTC_OV` **one component**, `LTC_GATE` one component, `Q3_CS` one component, VIN 4.910 mm and `R80.1`/`D12` connected: **the first board on which all of those hold simultaneously**, and **`LTC_OV` closed WITHOUT moving R77 or R78 at all**, so section 13's refinement budget was never spent. **A CONSTRAINT THE EARLIER ARITHMETIC HAD MISSED, AND IT IS WHY THE EXTRA D9 DISPLACEMENT IS NEEDED: THE CORRIDOR HAS TO FIT THE TRUNK, NOT JUST THE PAD.** The first chain found R75 legal at x = 4.125 with D9 moved only 0.200 mm, and the screen rejected `BAT_PROTECTED_P R75.2 -> D9.1` with `copper_edge_clearance 0.5000 mm; actual 0.4125 mm` - the pad cleared the edge, the **1.50 mm trunk leaving that pad** did not, because a 1.50 mm track centred on R75.2 needs that pad's CENTRE at least **1.250 mm** from the edge, which is **0.063 mm more than the +0.75 window can give**. **SECTION 17 THEREFORE APPLIES, AND THE EXACT NEW FIRST BLOCKER IS THIS: `BAT_PROTECTED_P R75.2 -> D9.1` is rejected at 1.50 mm on edge clearance and IS NEVER RETRIED AT D-249'S OWN 1.20 mm FLOOR.** `run()` walks the width ladder and stops the moment `connect_role` returns `ok`, and the DRC gate runs AFTER that - so a width that routes GEOMETRICALLY but fails the GATE is abandoned rather than falling to the next rung. `PLAN_1_BPP_TRUNK` carries `[1.50, 1.20]` precisely so the trunk can drop to its D-249 floor, and at the chosen pose that rung needs R75.x >= 4.063, which the +0.75 window (4.075 .. 4.150) satisfies. **The rung that would close the trunk is legal and the harness simply does not try it.** Recorded as **PR-49** and deliberately NOT fixed mid-stop, because it changes router behaviour board-wide. **THE SECOND BLOCKER IS THE ROUTED KELVIN DETOUR:** analytic **7.378 / 7.267** becomes routed **18.764 / 7.886**, over the 10 mm cap on one branch - a routing outcome rather than a placement one, since the placement is inside spec by a wide margin, and the same class FBV2-P2-002L recorded at 4.948 mm, now much larger. **THE TRADE, STATED PLAINLY: cluster +1.00 mm gives the 0.000 mm Kelvin window and COSTS THREE CONTROL PINS** - `LTC4368_FAULT_N`, `LTC_SHDN` and `LTC_GATE` all rejected on `BAT_MAIN routed clearance 0.3000 mm` at 0.2750, 0.2500 and 0.2778 mm, U18 down to 5 of 8 - **while cluster +0.75 mm keeps U18 at 8 of 8 with all nine targets and puts R75 in the window that cannot host a 1.50 mm trunk.** **SECTION 18'S SHUNT-CHANGE RESERVE IS NOT TRIGGERED**: it is conditioned on R75 still not physically fitting after the minimum legal D9 move, and **it fits** - the window was measured twice and the analytic mismatch inside it reaches 0.000 mm - so **no replacement-shunt requirement is produced and FBV2-P2-002O's suggestion that a shorter shunt might be needed is WITHDRAWN.** **DECISION REQUIRED: (a) AUTHORISE THE PR-49 LADDER-RETRY FIX** - let a width fall to its next rung when the DRC gate rejects a geometrically-successful route, which is board-wide router behaviour and therefore raised rather than taken; **(b) THEN CHOOSE THE CLUSTER SHIFT** - +0.75 mm (8 of 8, needs the 1.20 mm trunk rung) or +1.00 mm (0.000 mm Kelvin, costs three control pins); **(c) THE ROUTED KELVIN DETOUR NEEDS ATTENTION IN ITS OWN RIGHT** - 18.764 mm routed against 7.378 mm analytic, and no placement change addresses it. Nothing else was changed: R75's part number is untouched, Kelvin is not relaxed, Q3 did not move, the ECO was not broadened and the stack architecture is unchanged. `U11.2`'s flare is untouched and intact. **U19 NOT SEARCHED** (section 21); Phase A NOT run; no battery signal copper; converter routing NOT started. B-34 REMAINS OPEN. Full analysis: [`audits/2026-08-27-p2-002p-d261-d9.md`](audits/2026-08-27-p2-002p-d261-d9.md). **NO PROGRESS EARNED: PCB routing stays 0 %, overall stays 74 %.** | 2026-08-27 |
| D-263 | **FBV2-P2-002Q: PR-49 IS SETTLED AS A GENERAL ROUTER RULING, AND CLOSING THE TRUNK REVEALS A NEW CONTENTION. FAIL at section 14; the authoritative stackup was NOT changed**, because section 18 conditions the lock on section 14. The PCB is byte-identical to `adabe98` (md5 `a908cedfa9f9410aab327d8bd55b9f45`) - **four copper layers, zero signal tracks, zero signal vias** - and all five suites PASS. **PR-49, THE WIDTH-LADDER RULING, IS DELIVERED AND PROVEN TWICE ON REAL COPPER.** `run()` used to treat a rung as accepted the moment `connect_role` returned geometrically ok, with the DRC gate running afterwards - so a rung that routed and was then REJECTED abandoned the whole connection and the remaining rungs of an ALREADY AUTHORISED ladder were never tried. Now: **`BAT_PROTECTED_P R75.2 -> D9.1` routes at 1.50 mm, is rejected on `copper_edge_clearance`, falls to 1.20 mm and routes 19.219 mm on B.Cu with ZERO vias** - exactly the evidence section 6 predicted - and a second instance appeared unprompted, `LTC_GATE U18.10 -> Q3.4` falling 0.20 -> 0.15 mm. **`BAT_PROTECTED_P` now carries `D9.1` in its functional island**, closing the split FBV2-P2-002P reported. **THE RULE IS GENERAL AND ITS SAFETY BOUNDARY IS STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN A PROMISE:** it lives in one place, `route_battery_block.ladder_retry`, and only ever walks the ladder the path role ALREADY had - no rung is invented, nothing goes below a standing floor, no netclass or clearance is touched, DRC is never suppressed, and copper from a rejected rung is fully reverted before the next rung is attempted. `PLAN_1_BPP_TRUNK` remains exactly **1.50 mm target / 1.20 mm floor**, and **1.20 mm is used on `R75.2 -> D9.1` ALONE**, because that pose puts R75.2's centre 1.187 mm from the board edge while a 1.50 mm track centred there needs 1.250 mm - the D-249 floor, not a new concession. **A STANDING REGRESSION PINS IT: G9 in `router_regression.py`** proves the gate-rejected rung falls to the next authorised rung, that the retry **never invents a rung below the ladder**, that every-rung-rejected leaves the connection failed, that a **non-gate failure does NOT walk the ladder**, and that a rejected rung **leaves no copper on the board** (0 -> 5 -> 0 tracks); `router_regression` is ALL CHECKS PASS, G1-G9. **AND THE NEW FINDING, WHICH IS THE REASON THIS IS NOT A PASS: CLOSING THE TRUNK COSTS THREE U18 CONTROL PINS.** On the identical frozen 002P placement, 002P reported **U18 8 of 8 with the trunk ABSENT**; with PR-49 laying 19.219 mm of 1.20 mm trunk, **U18 falls to 5 of 8** - `U18.2`, `U18.3` and `U18.7` open. The two results do not conflict; they are the same board measured with one more connection on it, and **002P's 8 of 8 was in part an artefact of a connection that never got laid.** This is a contention of a kind none of 002M-002P faced: not a corridor, not a via, not a clearance rule, but **the high-current trunk and the LTC4368 pin field competing for the same lanes**, visible only once the trunk could actually be laid. **SECTION 9'S KELVIN-ORDERING HYPOTHESIS IS MEASURED FALSE**, exactly as section 9 required it be tested rather than assumed: routing the Kelvin taps before the trunk gives **U18 4 of 8** against 5 of 8 in plan order. The same-net argument is correct and beside the point - the taps do not obstruct the TRUNK, they take the pin-field lanes `LTC_UV`, `LTC_OV`, `LTC_SHDN` and `FAULT_N` need. The flag (`AQROOT_KELVIN_FIRST`) is left in the harness, OFF, so a rejected hypothesis stays reproducible instead of becoming folklore. **ROUTED KELVIN, BEST CASE: 8.667 / 11.130 mm, mismatch 2.463 mm** - the mismatch is comfortably inside 5.000 mm, **but `R75.2 -> U18.8` exceeds the 10.000 mm cap by 1.130 mm** and `R75.1 -> U18.9` takes an F.Cu excursion where section 8 prefers local B.Cu. Q3.3's POFV process remains approved and **its signal via was NOT written**. **B - D-263 ROUTING: DECISION STOP.** Section 10's reordering was implemented (`AQROOT_TRUNK_LAST`) and it **moves the casualty rather than removing it**: trunk-first (002Q) cost `U18.2`, `U18.3` and `U18.7`; Kelvin-first cost four control pins; **controls-first costs `U18.6` AND the sense pads** - `R75.1`, `R75.2`, `Q3.5` and `Q3.6` all `NO_LEGAL_ESCAPE`, leaving BAT_SENSE in five islands. **THREE ORDERS, THREE DIFFERENT CASUALTIES, ONE CAUSE: there is not enough B.Cu around U18 and R75 for the pin field, the sense pair and the high-current chain to coexist, and reordering only chooses who goes without.** With controls routed first the result is otherwise strong - **`LTC_OV` closes as ONE COMPONENT on B.Cu with ZERO vias** (`R77.2 -> R78.1` at 2.457 mm, the first time that has held in this order), `LTC_GATE` closes all six functional pads, `LTC4368_FAULT_N` all four, `LTC_UV` at 9.889 mm, VIN at 4.910 mm, `Q3_CS` one component through the POFV - **but `LTC_SHDN U18.6 -> R80.2` returns `NO_LEGAL_ESCAPE`**, so section 11's control-lane reservation never reaches 8 of 8 and cannot be captured. **AND SECTION 14'S PAIRED-INTERNAL KELVIN IS BARRED BY A STANDING RULE RATHER THAN BY GEOMETRY.** `BAT_PROTECTED_P U18.8 -> R75.2` on In2.Cu is rejected with **`Items not allowed (rule 'BAT_MAIN is outer-layer only')`**. That rule applies to the WHOLE net and the Kelvin tap is part of that net; it was written for the 1.5 A current path - at 0.5 oz an inner layer needs 2.73 mm for 1.5 A at a 10 K rise, which is the board's own arithmetic - and it makes **no exception for a nanoamp sense branch that merely shares the net name**. Mismatch 2.467 -> 2.283 mm.** Both on In2, paired, 2 vias each, no F.Cu, no POFV, **and no via site moved** - the same corridor, measured more carefully, and therefore not a relaxation of anything. **C - THE TAP PATH-ROLE CORRECTION IS DELIVERED, AND THE BOARD NARROWED THE RULING.** Via geometry is now selected by PATH ROLE, not by net: TRUNK 0.80/0.40, SIG 0.60/0.30, TAP/SENSE their own. **But a TAP on a POWER net CANNOT be given a small via, and every rejection proving it was correct:** 0.35/0.20 fails `via_diameter` (board minimum 0.50 mm), 0.50/0.25 fails `drill_out_of_range` (rule "POWER-class vias use the 0.40 mm drill"), 0.50/0.40 fails `annular_width` (rule "Via annular ring floor", 0.125 mm). **0.40 mm of drill plus two 0.125 mm rings is 0.65 mm, and that is the floor.** With **0.65/0.40 and OUTER LAYERS ONLY** - which keeps section 16's ban on a new `BAT_RAW` inner-layer exception intact - **`R79.1 -> R80.1` ROUTES at 4.532 mm**, and `D12.1 -> R77.1` improves from **28.058 mm at 0.60 mm to 13.682 mm at 0.30 mm on B.Cu with ZERO vias**. **Forbidding the hop outright was tried FIRST and measured WORSE than 002T** - `R77.1` AND `R79.1` both fell out of the island where 002T had kept `R77.1` - so section 16's "zero vias or stop" is the right instinct and the wrong mechanism; the outer-layer restriction is what carries its intent. `R77.1 -> R79.1` still fails `BAT_MAIN routed clearance`. **D - AND THE TRUNK, WHICH IS THE STOP.** `R75.2 -> (stage)` returns NO_PATH on both families, and the obstacle map says why in one line: **`R75.2` escapes at 1.50 mm, the staging point escapes at 1.50 mm in two directions, and there is NO B.Cu corridor between them at 1.50, 1.20, 1.00, 0.80, 0.60, 0.40, 0.30 OR 0.20 mm.** Straight-line distance 11.518 mm. **This is not width, not clearance, not a corridor family and not the length of the reservation: after the control field the control copper PARTITIONS B.Cu in the western margin. Nothing that reserves a PAD can repair a severed PLANE.** F2 additionally costs the `BAT_PROTECTED_P` closure routes F1 left alone. **E - AND THE OBVIOUS NEXT HYPOTHESIS IS MEASURED FALSE BEFORE IT COULD BE PROPOSED.** Same tree, same placement, same D-266 reservations, trunk EARLY: the trunk routes **19.219 mm at 1.20 mm** and **U18 falls to 5 of 8**, open on `U18.3`, `U18.7` and `U18.9`. **Reserving the sense exits does NOT make an early trunk affordable** - the trunk's own copper takes the lanes those pins need, exactly as 002Q measured before D-266 existed. So "reserve the whole trunk corridor" is early-trunk order wearing a new name, and it is now measured TWICE. **DECISION REQUIRED, AND THE FIELD IS NOW NARROW - THREE THINGS, NONE OF THEM THIS TASK'S TO CHOOSE: (a) A BOUNDED LAYER EXCEPTION FOR THE TRUNK WHERE IT CROSSES THE PIN FIELD** - one named corridor, In2 or In3, at a width the 0.5 oz inner copper can actually carry, which is a protection-architecture change to D-264; **(b) MOVE `TP17` AND/OR `C58` OUT OF THE WESTERN MARGIN** - a placement decision, and the only one that leaves every current path on outer 1 oz; **(c) ACCEPT A LONGER TRUNK THAT LEAVES THE MARGIN ENTIRELY** - the closure stage reached `D9.1` at 45.467 mm on an earlier screen, so a path exists at roughly 2.3x the length, with the resistance and area cost that implies. **SECTION 23 FAILS ON THE TRUNK AND ON THE `BAT_RAW` DIVIDER CHAIN AND PASSES ON EVERYTHING ELSE:** six-layer architecture, both GND planes, the frozen fingerprint, Q3.3 POFV with `Q3_CS` and `Q3_GATE`, **U18 8/8**, `LTC_SHDN`, `LTC_UV`, `LTC_OV` (B.Cu, zero vias), `FAULT_N` all four pads, `LTC_GATE` all six, the BAT_SENSE current path one island at 1.00 mm outer with zero vias, **both Kelvin branches inside 10 mm with mismatch 2.283 mm**, and `BAT_PROT_SHDN_CTL`. **Nothing was moved and nothing was relaxed:** D9, U18, R76..R83, R75 and Q3 frozen and fingerprint-asserted on every screen; no placement searched; **no netclass, clearance, annular or hole rule relaxed anywhere**; D-264 and D-266 untouched. **U19 NOT SEARCHED**; Phase A NOT run; Phase B NOT run; converter routing NOT started. The driver (`route_battery_block.py`) is scoped to "the whole battery / protection block" (the power tree) ONLY. **B — INTEGRITY BASELINE (re-verified this session).** `git rev-parse HEAD == origin/master == 01a38a5`, worktree clean; authoritative PCB `sha256 63a9bc54…f87d6ba9` / size 1475931; **432 tracks / 54 vias / 6 copper layers / 41 zones / 324 footprints**; `phaseA_journal.json` **77 entries**; **every one of the 432 routed tracks is an in-scope power-tree net (0 out-of-scope) → the board carries Phase-A battery-block copper ONLY**; `router_regression.py` = **ALL 79 CHECKS PASS (G1–G17)**; the shared journal was not mutated. **C — THE EXISTING PHASE-B DRIVERS ASSUME A COPPER-EMPTY BASE (the sharply-characterized blocker, proven).** (i) `replay_battery_block.py:40-42` hard-refuses a non-empty authoritative board (`"authoritative board already carries N track items" → raise SystemExit`); post-D-302 the board carries 432 tracks, so it can never run again — but its promotion role is ALREADY fulfilled byte-identically by D-302's direct scratch copy. (ii) SECTION-17 `AQROOT_REPLAY` (`route_battery_block.py:2297`) SKIPS every `role=='TRUNK+ESCAPE'` journal entry — which is EXACTLY the ONE entry the D-302 lever added to CLOSE the terminal wall and DEFINE the promotion: `BAT_PROTECTED_P U11.2→C36.1, w=1.5, reinforcement=True`. A replay would carry **76 of 77** journal items, dropping the wall closure, and would NOT reproduce the promoted board (it would re-hit the U11.2 wall or diverge). (iii) `phaseB_compare.py` requires a `phaseB.json` that was NEVER produced — no reproduction/compare was ever wired or run for this board (the 004b2 promotion went scratch→authoritative directly). The D-271 "independent reproduction" proof is therefore a MODEST-value nicety whose machinery is stale; re-running it as-is is not justified (a long, divergence-prone route that would demonstrably drop the U11 closure). **E — THE REAL REMAINING PHASE-B (scoped, the next bounded lever).** Rest-of-board = **164 multi-pad nets, 0 routed**, spanning 9 subsystem sheets + rails (largest: GND 259 pads, +3V3 86 pads; groups 09_COMMUNITY_HEADER 20 nets, 04_SPI_B_RADIOS_NFC 20, 01_POWER_TREE-beyond-block 18, top-level 17, 08_BUTTONS_EXPANDERS 10, …). This is ~85 % of remaining routing and has NO driver (route_battery_block is power-tree only). The next lever is a NEW, scoped, INCREMENTAL rest-of-board driver that LOADS the promoted board, PRESERVES the accepted Phase-A power-tree copper (never erase/reroute — accepted invariant), routes a bounded isolated net-group first, is gated by real full-board DRC (D-286), and promotes only a genuine no-casualty / no-new-DRC increment; the wholesale-copy `replay_battery_block.py` must be generalized for incremental promotion (copper-empty-base guard removed) or a new incremental promoter written. **F — OPPORTUNITY & SIMPLIFICATION SCAN (mandated, routing-phase transition).** Routing the rest of the board is core CTO-authority engineering, not an owner decision (no product feature/scope/safety/cost/topology change). Opportunity: decompose by sheet/net-group so each increment is small, real-DRC-gated and independently promotable (matches the D-297 "bare inner In2/In3 signal layers are spare capacity" lesson and the direction-2 escape corridors). Simplification: the stale one-shot replay path should be retired/generalized rather than carried as a trap. No BOM/recoverability/testability/firmware/UX opportunity forces a change; the six-layer stack, GND planes (In1/In4), footprints, outline, DNP/tuning provisions are all frozen and adequate. **Open owner decisions: NONE.** **G — INTEGRITY.** No copper/placement/rule/floor/DRU/topology/footprint/outline change; authoritative PCB byte-identical (`sha256 63a9bc54…f87d6ba9`); shared journal at HEAD; D-249 (≥1.20 mm BPP), D-269 (0.300 mm), D-257 via ladder, 0.60 mm BAT_MAIN, 0.200/0.150 signal, 0.25 hole-hole, D-275/D-288 bridge ENFORCED; D-290 untouched; the accepted `AQROOT_U18BPP_JOIN` (D-297), `AQROOT_U19CAP` (D-299/G14), `AQROOT_LTCGATE_KO` (D-301/G15), `AQROOT_U11_RETARGET` (D-302/G16), the D-302 fixture split (G17), `place_003l` (D-285), D-275 and D-277..D-302 all preserved; frozen `beta-full-reference-v1` untouched. The same `incremental_router.py`, minimally extended, loaded the D-305 promoted board, routed the DISP_RST_N display-reset control net (1 net, 3 pads: R16.1/J1.10 F.Cu + U2.8 B.Cu) with a pure-F.Cu run (R16.1↔J1.10) AND one board-legal F↔B through via (J1.10↔U2.8, 0.60/0.30 Default netclass), re-poured ONLY the In1/In4 GND planes to open the via's anti-pad, WITHOUT touching a single strand of accepted Phase-A / FRONT_RGB / ACC copper, and a real full-board gate proved a GENUINE no-casualty / no-new-DRC connectivity increment (ratsnest 697→695, DRC unchanged) → COPPER PROMOTED; AUTONOMY CONTINUES, no owner decision, D-275 AND D-277..D-305 PRESERVED.** Starting HEAD `c22b9fd` (D-305; pushed; `origin/master` identical). A GOVERNED CTO ACCEPT + PROMOTE (routine rest-of-board routing within CTO authority; **no owner decision raised**). **A — GROUP SELECTION (measured, prefer a new safe primitive).** Baseline `f0046eb7…` (483/54/6, ratsnest 697, journal 84); candidates screened (`w/screen_007.py`, READ-ONLY) by pad geometry / layer-THT / Phase-A congestion. CHOSEN: **DISP_RST (`/DISP_RST_N`)** — one 3-pad net with pads NOT all on one layer (R16.1/J1.10 F.Cu SMD, U2.8 B.Cu SMD): its MST is one SAME-LAYER edge (R16.1↔J1.10, the **first incremental F.Cu run**) + one CROSS-LAYER edge (J1.10↔U2.8, the **first incremental via / mixed-layer route**, ONE 0.60/0.30 Default through via ≥ the 0.50 mm `min_via_diameter` — not a microvia, not a via-in-pad). Low congestion (2 Phase-A items in bbox+2 mm), NONCRITICAL low-speed reset line. REJECTED: AUDIO_SPK (isolated F.Cu+THT but SPK_P/N are class-D SWITCHING outputs the mandate excludes); U11_PROG (16 items, coupled to the safety-critical BPP path); PWR_SENSE (12 items, west fuel-gauge congestion). FALLBACK held (not needed): IMU_STRAP `BMI270_SDO_ADDR` B.Cu singleton — DISP_RST was not disproven, so the simple-B.Cu fallback stays a future increment. EXCLUDED per mandate: community-header/RF/NFC/USB/crystals/GND/+3V3 rails. **B — THE NEW-PRIMITIVE BLOCKER, CHARACTERISED (not brute-forced).** The through via pierces the In1/In4 GND reference planes; the stale plane fill (poured before the via) had no anti-pad, so the first gate saw `clearance` ×2 + `hole_clearance` ×2 at (52.95,87.0) vs the In1/In4 GND zones. Focused evidence bounded the fix: a plain refill drifts ONLY zones 39/40 (In1/In4 GND, +35 poly-pts each — a stored-vs-current `ZONE_FILLER` discrepancy independent of the via) and NO other zone. So `route` now re-pours EXACTLY In1/In4 when (and only when) a via was laid; DRC returns to baseline IDENTICALLY. Plane byte-equality is NOT claimed (the stored fill is not `ZONE_FILLER`-reproducible) — the promotable standard is DRC-neutral + "only In1/In4 changed", proven by the gate. **C — THE EXPERIMENT (one foreground run).** `route DISP_RST` → 2/2 ok (R16.1↔J1.10 10.881 mm F.Cu; J1.10↔U2.8 18.081 mm F.Cu run + 0.60/0.30 through via at (52.950,87.000) + B.Cu stub; 11 segments + 1 via); scratch 494/55, AUTH sha UNCHANGED. `gate DISP_RST` = **PASS, every check** (Phase-A 0 missing; 12 new items all target-net; ONLY In1/In4 GND planes re-poured, all other 39 zones identical; DISP_RST_N fully connected open-edges 2→0; 0 prior pairs regressed; ratsnest 697→695 EXACTLY −2; no new/worse DRC; unc 499→499). **D — WHAT IS PROMOTED (integrity).** Authoritative `sha256 f0046eb7…04c7cd41` → **`9c0586d8…e3f62259`**; tracks **483→494** (+11 DISP_RST_N); vias **54→55** (+1 F↔B through via); 6 layers / 41 zones unchanged; ratsnest **697→695** (−2); journal **84→86** (+2 REST_INC); board diff **470 ins / 336 del** — ALL 336 deletions are In1/In4 `filled_polygon` xy (the plane re-pour); ZERO deleted `segment`/`via`/`footprint` lines (grep-confirmed); real KiCad DRC **identical** (`{solder_mask_bridge:1, hole_clearance:5, lib_footprint_issues:199, unconnected_items:499}`). Every 432 Phase-A + 20 FRONT_RGB + 31 ACC track + all 54 prior vias present byte/geometry-identical; the two GND planes keep net/layer/priority/clearance — only their fill re-poured to carry the via anti-pad. No placement/DRU/netclass/footprint/value/polarity/outline/stackup change. **E — TESTS.** New contract **G20** pins the increment (DISP_RST_N connected across the F/B hop; copper legal = 11 trk spanning F.Cu+B.Cu at 0.200 mm + exactly one 0.60/0.30 through via; ADD-ONLY = RGB 20 + ACC 31 + Phase-A 432/54). **G18/G19 generalised** — their ADD-ONLY via count now pins `phaseA_via`==54 (vias NOT owned by any REST_INC net) instead of `all_via`==54, so the Phase-A-vias pin survives increments that add vias (no change to their RGB/ACC claims). `router_regression.py` = **ALL 89 CHECKS PASS (G1–G20)**, run twice, deterministic. New probe `incremental_probe_008.py` ALL PASS; `incremental_probe_006/007.py` refreshed to the D-306 board (`_007` generalised) ALL PASS; `phaseB_bringup_probe_005.py` updated (494/55/86; accepted set FRONT_RGB + ACC_3V3_CTL + DISP_RST_N; 164 rest nets, 6 routed, 158 unrouted) ALL PASS. **F — OPPORTUNITY & SIMPLIFICATION SCAN (mandated).** The first via increment gained EXACTLY three generic mechanics, each forced by a concrete need: a per-edge layer decision (`edge_plan` — same-layer groups stay byte-identical), a `connect_cross` helper composing only proven `qrouter` primitives (escape → via_site → via → two anchored `connect_role` runs — **`qrouter.py` untouched**, so the battery driver is unaffected), and a `refill_planes` step re-pouring only In1/In4 when a via was laid. The gate already fingerprinted vias generically; only a zone-preservation proof was added. Deliberately NOT generalised: no via-ladder abstraction, no blind/buried/microvia path, no multi-via arrays — those await the increment that forces them. No BOM/recoverability/testability/firmware/UX/mechanical change forced; In2/In3 remain spare. **Open owner decisions: NONE.** **G — INTEGRITY & ROLLBACK.** Rollback = pre-promotion `sha256 f0046eb7…04c7cd41` (D-305; parent `c22b9fd`; restored by `git checkout` of the PCB + journal; D-302/D-304/D-305 rollback points still stand). All locked invariants preserved: no DRU/rule/clearance/stackup/topology/net/footprint/value/polarity/outline change; no D-290 reauth; NO via below the D-257 ladder (the 0.60/0.30 through via IS the Default netclass geometry, ≥ 0.50 mm min_via); D-249 (≥1.20 mm BPP), D-269 (0.300 mm), 0.60 mm BAT_MAIN, 0.200/0.150 signal, 0.25 hole-hole, D-275/D-288 bridge, **In1/In4 GND roles** (planes stay GND — only fill re-poured), USB/RF/mechanical reservations ENFORCED; `AQROOT_U18BPP_JOIN` (D-297), `AQROOT_U19CAP` (D-299/G14), `AQROOT_LTCGATE_KO` (D-301/G15), `AQROOT_U11_RETARGET` (D-302/G16), fixture split (G17), FRONT_RGB (G18/D-304), ACC_3V3_CTL (G19/D-305), `place_003l` (D-285), D-275 and D-277..D-305 preserved; frozen `beta-full-reference-v1` untouched; DEVICE_SPEC unchanged (no hardware fact changed); shared journal authoritative (86 entries); no orphan process. **H — NEXT FBV2-P2-009:** continue rest-of-board routing via the same framework (158 of 164 rest nets unrouted); both same-layer and single-via cross-layer groups are now proven. Good candidates: IMU_STRAP `BMI270_SDO_ADDR` (the held B.Cu fallback), short isolated 08_BUTTONS controls, or another short mixed-layer control; the first increment needing MULTIPLE vias / a via array / an In2/In3 inner-signal traverse should extend `connect_cross`/`refill_planes` deliberately (the current helper handles exactly one via + the In1/In4 re-pour). Defer RF/NFC/USB/community-header/rails. A GOVERNED CTO ACCEPT + PROMOTE (routine rest-of-board routing within CTO authority; **no owner decision raised**). **A — ROOT CAUSE OF THE D-309 WALL: the router was BLIND to existing vias.** `qrouter.QBoard._scan` builds obstacles from footprint pads + `PCB_TRACK` but iterates `GetTracks()` and `continue`s on `PCB_VIA` — every accepted through-via is invisible to escape/via_site/connect_role. U2.4/.7/.8/.11 stack on U2's WEST edge (x=54.14); the accepted D-306 `DISP_RST_N` via sits at (52.95,87.0), 1.19 mm west of that column, so a westward cross-layer escape lands the new via (and threads its F.Cu run) right past the barrel; only real DRC caught it (D-309 +3; measured this cycle `AMP_SD_MODE` default via 0.100 mm copper to `DISP_RST_N`). **B — THE FIX: two generic, bounded, qrouter-UNTOUCHED mechanisms in `connect_cross`.** (1) EXISTING-VIA AWARENESS — every accepted `PCB_VIA` barrel/hole is injected as an obstacle onto the per-route `QBoard` instance (mirroring `QBoard.via()` item-for-item), so escape/via_site/**connect_role's track search** respect accepted vias; add-only, per-route, generic; it can only make a route MORE conservative and never deletes/alters copper, and it touches only the transient route QBoard so the G-contract fixtures (which build their own QBoards through `qrouter`) are unaffected. (2) BOUNDED VIA-SITE OFFSET — a group opts in with `via_offset` and the F↔B transition is deliberately walked ~2.5 mm off the nearest congesting barrel via `_offset_via_site` (a short host-face B.Cu fan-out) — the first increment that PLANS a via site rather than accepting the router's first via-blind legal one; groups without `via_offset` are byte-identical to D-306/D-308. **C — SCREEN (real-geometry clearance, before any gate).** READ-ONLY `w/geom_012.py` + `w/screen_012.py` reproduced `cmd_route`'s cross-layer edge and measured DEFAULT vs bounded offset sites against REAL existing-via copper+hole (qrouter-blind): `AMP_SD_MODE` default via 0.70 mm from DISP = **0.100 mm CLASH** (confirms D-309 +7); `TOUCH_RST_N`/`SD_DETECT` default vias clear the barrel but their tracks thread the west column (D-309 +3/+2); at 2.5 mm offset all four clear comfortably (via↔via 2.6–7.8 mm) with fan-out ≤2.8 mm off the U2 pad. `TOUCH_INT_N` is on U2's EAST edge, already 5.9 mm clear. Per the task preference the coherent display/touch PAIR was taken (both pass); unrelated nets NOT bundled. **D — ROUTE→GATE→PROMOTE.** `route TOUCH_CTL` ALL OK (injected 58 existing-via obstacles): J1.47↔R12.1 22.217 mm F.Cu + R12.1↔U2.4 28.553 mm cross-via@(52.95,92.10); J1.46↔U2.19 54.708 mm cross-via@(61.15,88.85); 26 seg 0.200 mm + 2 through vias 0.60/0.30; In1/In4 [39,40] re-poured. (First attempt with via-offset ALONE still failed +3 — the via-blind track router threaded the F.Cu run 0.05 mm from the DISP barrel; the existing-via injection made connect_role via-aware and the re-route was clean — the offset fixes the via, the injection fixes the tracks.) `gate` PASS every check (D-309 535 trk+58 via SUBSET; 28 new items all target-net; only zones 39/40 re-poured, all other 39 byte-identical; both nets connected open-edges 2→0 and 1→0; 0 prior pairs regressed; ratsnest 688→685 EXACTLY −3; **no new/worse DRC class, `clearance` 0→0**; unc 499→499); `promote` re-ran gate PASS, re-verified AUTH sha undrifted, merged 3 REST_INC. **E — PROMOTED.** `sha256 5c5cae79…a339f63` → **`856f7a8adf0db9b114b9f09d7469308f921bc897aaf2ddce7f1c15c40a197114`**; tracks 535→**561** (+26: 21 F.Cu + 5 B.Cu fan-out); vias 58→**60** (+2 offset through vias); 6 layers / 41 zones; ratsnest 688→**685** (−3); journal 93→**96** (+3 REST_INC); PCB diff **310 ins / 40 del** — additions 26 `(segment)` + 2 `(via)` (0 seg/via/fp del, grep-confirmed); all 40 dels are In1/In4 `filled_polygon` xy (2 via anti-pads), nothing else. Real KiCad DRC error-severity identical (`solder_mask_bridge:1 + hole_clearance:5`; 0 `clearance`). **F — TESTS.** New **G24** pins the increment (both nets connected across the U2 F/B hop; copper legal 26 trk 0.200 F.Cu+B.Cu + 2×0.60/0.30 vias; **offset cleared both vias of every existing via — min TOUCH-via↔other-via 4.998 mm ≥0.80 mm**; ADD-ONLY IR_RX_VS 8+RGB_LED 25+IMU 8+DISP 11+ACC 31+RGB 20+Phase-A 432/54). | D-325 | **FBV2-P2-027: THE DUPLICATE-REF MST FRAMEWORK FIX LANDED AND THE FIRST SWx USER-BUTTON NET `BTN_B_N` IS ROUTED AND PROMOTED (the NINETEENTH rest-of-board increment) — a bounded, generic, deterministic change lets the MST + gate see a footprint's TWO same-numbered physical lands as distinct nodes, so the whole user-button family is now routable. A real full-board gate proved a GENUINE no-casualty / no-new-DRC increment → COPPER PROMOTED; AUTONOMY CONTINUES, no owner decision, D-275 AND D-277..D-324 PRESERVED.** Starting HEAD `45f45bc` (D-324; pushed; `origin/master` identical; AUTH `a7bf8bdc…`, 781/68, ratsnest 665, journal 116). A GOVERNED CTO ACCEPT + PROMOTE. **A — PRECISE ROOT-CAUSE DIAGNOSIS.** `SW7` (Button_Switch_SMD:SW_SPST_PTS645Sx43SMTR92) is a 4-pin tact switch whose TWO mechanically-linked terminals BOTH carry pad NUMBER "1" on `BTN_B_N` at DIFFERENT physical locations — measured `(49.520,96.750)` and `(57.480,96.750)`, 7.96 mm apart (and two pad "2" on GND likewise). `qrouter.QBoard._scan` keys its pad table `self.pads[(net,"REF.NUM")]`, so the SECOND "SW7.1" write OVERWROTE the first and one physical land was invisible to `net_pads()`/the MST; D-323 routed only one terminal and the real full-board gate FAILed on connectivity (`open_edges 2→1`). Independently, `cmd_gate`'s `net_open_edges()` ref-deduped the two lands in its own cluster count — a MATCHING collapse that under-counted the net's owed ratsnest edges. **B — THE BOUNDED, GENERIC, DETERMINISTIC FIX (incremental_router.py only; qrouter.py UNTOUCHED so every G-contract that routes through QBoard stays byte-identical).** `physical_net_pads()` sources MST nodes by stable PHYSICAL identity `(ref, x, y)` — every distinct land is its own node; ORDINARY unique-pad nets return the exact `net_pads()` dict objects (verified: SAME objects), so their routing/journal is byte-unchanged. `cmd_gate.net_open_edges()` was rewritten as a physical-pad union-find (keyed by `(ref,x,y)`, copper adjacency from `GetConnectedItems`), so it counts copper clusters over PHYSICAL lands — matching KiCad's own ratsnest, which owes one edge per physical land. The change ADDS nodes only for a genuinely duplicated pad number; it weakens NO rule, netclass, clearance, via geometry, or topology. **C — ROUTE + GATE + PROMOTE.** `BTN_B_N` (navigation/boot button `SW7.1` F.Cu / pull-up `R9.2` B.Cu / expander `U2.18` B.Cu) MST hubbed on `R9.2` → BOTH `SW7.1` lands (two 0.60/0.30 Default THROUGH vias in the OPEN south button field at `(48.300,96.750)` and `(56.300,95.600)`, In1/In4 GND planes re-poured once for the two anti-pads) + one SAME-LAYER B.Cu run `R9.2→U2.18`. > **The 915 MHz external whip antenna MPN is not selected.** Everything from the module socket to
> the panel bulkhead is now locked and orderable; the antenna that screws onto the outside is not.
> It is an accessory-class purchase with **no board impact**, but a range test means nothing
> without it. Pick a standard 868/915 MHz SMA-male whip at procurement.
>
> **Nothing else was added:** no new features, no new rails, no new connectors, no speculative
> fallback circuitry, no PGOOD IC, no I²C mux, no PCB change, and no passive-value consolidation —
> that last one deliberately, because consolidating values before the layout exists optimises the
> wrong thing.

## 9. Safety

| # | decision | date |
|---|---|---|
| D-070 | **Full Beta v2 may not be released to fabrication with unresolved power / self-damage blockers.** | 2026-08-22 |
| D-071 | **Reverse-polarity protection remains a fabrication blocker until resolved.** `BAT_CONNECTOR_P` is currently a single-pad dead net; nothing bridges it to `BAT_PROTECTED_P`. | 2026-08-22 |
| D-072 | **Power / backfeed / NFC-boost / accessory-power / thermal / footprint gates are mandatory.** If physical Beta-DM bring-up is skipped, these become fab-blocking rather than nice-to-have, because there is no earlier board on which they could fail cheaply. | 2026-08-22 |

| ~~superseded~~ | ~~**Dead-cell recovery: Candidate B or Candidate D?**~~ **B** — a *hardware-qualified* precharge from `SYS` to `BAT_RAW`, gated by a GND-referenced comparator interlock so a reversed cell draws ≈0 A; no firmware, works with a corrupted image; ~8–10 parts, 1–3 µA. **D** — no recovery path; deeply discharged packs are serviced. **Recommendation: B for the product, D acceptable for the first five boards.** | **THE ONLY ITEM BLOCKING FBV2-A1.** A single MOSFET cannot distinguish 0 V from −3.7 V — both turn it *more* on — so an explicit level-sensing element is mandatory and this is a genuine new power-tree branch. *(Supersedes the earlier firmware-gated proposal: the CTO prefers safety not to depend on firmware, and that is the better position.)* | 2026-08-22 |

It survives only as a double-fault consideration and is no longer an architecture item. | schematic-phase note only | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**P-13**~~ | ~~Latch-off vs hot-insertion inrush.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-22 by FBV2-PWR-001.** The LTC4368 datasheet gives `I_INRUSH = (C_OUT/C_GATE) × I_GATE(UP)` and the design rule `I_OC,FWD > I_INRUSH + I_OUT` — inrush is designed, ≈350 mA against a 3.33 A trip. Separately, **RETRY latch-off applies to FORWARD overcurrent only**; reverse faults reconnect automatically once VOUT falls 100 mV below VIN. Both halves of the objection fall away. | closed | 2026-08-22 |

With AWS the VDD_AM capacitor changes to 10–50 nF. Schematic-time decision, product call. | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**P-18**~~ | ~~Accessory I2C segmentation - buffer alone, or add a mux?~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-23 by D-178 (FBV2-S1-009). NO MUX.** The bus-hang half was already closed by the detect-gated rail; the **TCA4307** now closes it structurally, with autonomous stuck-bus disconnect and up to 16 recovery clocks (D-176). **Address collision is not an electrical problem and a mux is the wrong tool for it**: the external segment stays one logical address space with the internal bus, and allocation is governed by the normative [`architecture/I2C_ADDRESS_REGISTRY.md`](architecture/I2C_ADDRESS_REGISTRY.md). `0x50` is NOT widened; P-19 stays future protocol scope. | closed |
| ~~**P-10**~~ | ~~NFC supply topology.~~ **N1** — run NFC entirely at 3.3 V (`sup3V` option bit; VDD range 2.4–3.6 V) and **delete** U13, L2, R44, R45, C19, C34, C35, C55; or **N2** — keep the 5 V boost and never disable it while the system is on. Created by the DS12484 finding that VDD and VDD_TX cannot be split. | With true load disconnect confirmed on the TPS61023, disabling the boost leaves VDD = 0 V while VDD_IO = 3.3 V — a state the datasheet nowhere authorises. **N1 recommended**: deletes a converter, eight parts, the OVP question and the sequencing question. Price is RF range. | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**M-06**~~ | ~~Display MPN and FPC interface not locked.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-23 by D-074…D-078 (FBV2-DISP-002).** `ER-TFT035IPS-6` + `ER-TPC035-6`; 50-pin, 0.5 mm, bottom contact, 0.30 ± 0.03 mm; FT6236 @ 0x38; `J1` = Hirose `FH69-50S-0.5SH`. | closed | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**M-07**~~ | ~~Backlight driver re-derivation.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-23 by D-079.** TPS61169 retained; `R69` = 1.87 R, `R70`–`R73` = 4 x 33 R; switch-peak margin 4.6x; `L3`/`D8`/`C44` unchanged. | closed | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**M-01**~~ | ~~Display size / panel MPN.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-22 by D-072 - 3.5 inch ruled.** Replaced by M-06. | closed | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~superseded~~ | ~~**Display size / panel MPN.**~~ 2.8″ CH280QV10-CT is inherited from Beta-DM, and its exact outline, thickness and FPC bend stack **are not archived locally**. A 50 × 69 mm module in an 80 × 160 front is modest — the cavity comfortably accepts **3.2″ or 3.5″**. | Does **not** block FBV2-A2 or schematic migration. **Does** block PCB floorplanning (FBV2-P1): display size sets the front layout, which sets the rear free area, which sets the NFC zone. | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**M-02**~~ | ~~Battery capacity target.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-22 by D-071** - 60 x 75 x 8.0 mm, ~2500-3000 mAh. | closed | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~superseded~~ | ~~**Battery capacity target.**~~ The confirmed 3.5 mm of Z margin supports an **8.0 mm** pack (~2500–3000 mAh) against the **2000 mAh** assumed in the power budget. | Either answer fits. Confirm the target, then re-derive runtime in [[13 - Power Budget and Battery Runtime v0.1]]. | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~**P-07**~~ | ~~Exact mechanical internal cavity.~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-22 by FBV2-MECH-001** — cavity derived at **75.0 × 155.0 × 18.5 mm** and recorded as TARGET in the mechanical interface spec. | closed | 2026-08-22 |
| ~~superseded~~ | ~~**Exact mechanical internal cavity.**~~ Internal cavity X/Y/Z, wall thickness and PCB-to-wall clearance have **never existed** in this repository. | Blocks the v2 outline, and therefore all placement and routing. **Now the long-pole item.** | 2026-08-22 |

### How a pending decision closes

A pending item closes by being moved into a numbered `D-xxx` row above, with its
date, and by an entry in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md). It is removed from this
table only when it has a home in the locked sections.
# D-447 · 2026-09-02 · Demo ISET package-land wall bounded; local-route promotion guard hardened

The retained BQ25185 charge-current programming net `/01_POWER_TREE/ISET`
remains open between R37.1 and U11.8.  The qualified 0.20 mm B.Cu local router
finds no legal escape from U11.8: stable blockers are U11.6 (22 samples), U11.9
(22), accepted track geometry (6), and the board edge (5).  No candidate copper
is emitted and the authoritative board remains byte-identical at `7a764bac...`;
real refilled schematic-parity DRC remains at the accepted 199 footprint-library
/ 5 hole-clearance / 1 solder-mask-bridge signature.

The adjacent `ILIM_VSET` fallback exposed a reusable gate defect: that net was
already one fitted copper island, but the local router could reproduce five
coincident segments with new UUIDs.  The candidate was rejected, the board was
restored exactly, and `route_local_two_pad.py` now requires a strict fitted
open-edge reduction in addition to route success and clean DRC before writing
or promoting a candidate.  Regression proves `ILIM_VSET` now reports 0→0 and
is non-promotable, while ISET reports 1→1 and remains non-promotable.  D-269,
D-186, all three RGB replacements, XGPIO4/XGPIO5, approved Demo NC contacts,
accepted NFC copper, and `hardware/beta-v2/` remain unchanged.  No owner
decision is open.  **Next:** bound a U11-local escape transaction for ISET that
atomically reserves the adjacent U11.6/U11.9 branches before U11.8, without
moving U11 or weakening 0.20 mm clearance; if empty, park the package pocket
and freshly rank an independent retained net.

# D-448 · 2026-09-02 · Demo U11 adjacent-branch ordering family exhausted

The D-447 successor deterministically screens both U11.6 (`TS_MR`) / U11.9
(`STAT1`) reservation orders and all eight 2 mm directional targets per land
before attempting U11.8 (`ISET`): 128 scratch-only cases at the locked 0.20 mm
B.Cu width and clearance. U11.6 reserves successfully in all 64 TS-first cases.
U11.9 returns `NO_LEGAL_ESCAPE` in all 64 STAT1-first cases and after every
successful TS reservation, so zero case can reach ISET and no candidate copper
is emitted. This disproves branch ordering as the in-place package-pocket lever.
The authoritative board remains byte-identical at `7a764bac...`; D-269, D-186,
the three RGB replacements, XGPIO4/XGPIO5, approved Demo NCs, accepted NFC
copper, and `hardware/beta-v2/` remain unchanged. Park the U11 package pocket;
next freshly rank an independent retained local net or coherent local cluster.
No owner decision is open.
